Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on coding structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 889. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 129 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_889(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 889. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^129(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the coding structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 25. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 915 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to coding theory, we decompose the problem into 25 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 915. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
